租户配额再平衡：接口与业务规格\r\n\r\n修复缺失实现，依据真实输入生成可重建、确定性的JSON产物。以下业务算法的节点、边、窗口或资源是被实现程序的数据语义，不是Agent执行计划。\r\n\r\n## 实现范围与保护边界\r\n\r\n组件目录为`engines/rebalance_windows/`、`engines/resource_order/`、`engines/operator_assignment/`。每个组件实现model.py、transform.py、render.py；根目录实现run_all.py、final_report.py。可添加必要的私有辅助模块。\r\n不得修改README.md、任务说明、inputs/、任何contract.json、check_module.py、根check.py或integrate.py。已有受保护稳定接口保留。\r\n\r\n## 公共函数接口与输入解释\r\n\r\n- `model.load_payload(path: Path)`：读取UTF-8 JSON，对象或数组顶层合法；非法JSON、顶层null/布尔/数字/字符串均抛ValueError。业务字段由transform检查。\r\n- `transform.transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`：payloads严格按该契约inputs的顺序传入已读取的对象或数组，返回`{\"name\": contract[\"name\"], \"result\": 算法结果对象}`。输出恰有name/result两层；本说明各operation的输出字段均属于result，不得额外嵌套一次name/result。不得修改payloads或contract。\r\n- 输入对象仅当键集合恰好是`{\"name\",\"result\"}`时视为上游组件封装；先读取其result，再使用对应operation约定的字段。原始本地对象不是文件路径，也不当成records或nodes列表。多输入operation必须消费全部指定输入，不能复制固定上游结果。\r\n- `render.build_component(root: Path, contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\r\n- int字段严格排除bool；有限数字允许int/float但排除bool、NaN、Infinity。非空文本先检查去首尾空白后不为空；是否返回去空白/小写形式由各operation明确规定，不全局改写身份字段。列表与字典按规定类型读取，不把null当空集合，不自动把数字字符串转换成数字。\r\n- 文中没有给出默认值的必要字段缺失、非法类型或明确列出的冲突抛ValueError；合法无解使用operation的无解结果，不等同于输入错误。词典序采用通常字符串/列表/元组顺序，相同前缀时较短列表更小。\r\n- contract中的name/operation/inputs/output是接口字段；只有下列业务规则显式使用的算法参数参与计算，不能看到threshold便对所有operation筛选。参数值读取本组件契约，不从样例推断固定常量。\r\n\r\n## 各组件的完整业务规则\r\n\r\n### engines/rebalance_windows — 带冷却期的加权区间选择\r\n\r\noperation：`weighted_intervals`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\r\n\r\n输入首对象{windows:[{id:非空str,start:int>=0,end:int>start,value:int}]}，契约cooldown:int>=0。id去首尾空白后唯一；重复id即ValueError。半开区间相容条件为较早窗口end+cooldown<=较晚窗口start。输出result={selected:[排序后的规范化id],value:所选value之和}。优化顺序：总value最大；再比较排序后的整个ID列表，按通常字典序取最小，较短的相同前缀列表更小。允许负value和0值，允许空集合；若最优价值为0，空列表优于所有非空同价值列表。不得只给DP局部状态按单个ID打破平局，也不能仅因窗口value为0就一概排除它——它可能改变正价值最优解的完整ID列表平局。空windows为selected=[]、value=0。本operation只消费首输入，契约threshold不参与目标。\r\n\r\n局部检查命令：`python3 engines/rebalance_windows/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\r\n\r\n### engines/resource_order — 依赖图与确定性关键路径\r\n\r\noperation：`critical_path`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\r\n\r\n首输入{nodes:[{id:非空str,duration:int>=0,deps:list[id]}]}。节点id去首尾空白后唯一，节点输出id为去空白值；依赖引用必须准确匹配这些id。重复id/依赖、未知依赖、自依赖和环均ValueError。后续组件result若有feasible字段：false直接返回{blocked:true,waves:[],critical_path:[],makespan:0,earliest_finish:{}}；true则读取assignments中的job/worker，同一worker的job按jobid升序，相邻job添加先后依赖。新增边也必须无环；未知被分配job报错；未分配节点仍按原依赖存在。没有分配结果时绝不凭空添加人员依赖。每波取本波开始时全部就绪节点并按id排序；finish=duration+最大前驱finish。最大finish的前驱并列时，比较前驱已经形成的完整路径（不含当前节点）取字典序最小，然后追加当前节点；最后在最大finish终点间按其完整路径列表取最小。正常result={blocked:false,waves,critical_path,makespan,earliest_finish}，earliest_finish按id；空图的两个列表与映射为空、makespan=0。不要把分配结果封装的name/result两层误当作assignments。\r\n\r\n局部检查命令：`python3 engines/resource_order/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\r\n\r\n### engines/operator_assignment — 技能容量约束分配\r\n\r\noperation：`capacity_assignment`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\r\n\r\n首输入{jobs:[{id,skill:非空str,duration:int>=1}],workers:[{id,skills:list[非空str],capacity:int>=0,cost:int>=0,affinity:{job_id:int}}]}。分别检查jobs/workers的id为非空文本且去首尾空白后的id不重复；实际标识、技能匹配、输出及排序按原字符串使用，不进行大小写转换。每个job必须分配恰好一次，worker必须有完全相同的skill，且所分配duration之和<=capacity。后续输入逐个解开组件result：存在selected键时，仅保留id位于selected中的worker（selected不是job列表）；存在makespan键时，把每个剩余worker.capacity缩为min(capacity,makespan)，不是总容量。可行解依次最大化sum(worker.affinity.get(job.id,0))，最小化sum(worker.cost*job.duration)，最后按job升序形成(job,worker)元组列表并取字典序最小。result={feasible,score,cost,assignments:[{job,worker}]}，assignments按job排序。无可行分配为false/0/0/[]；没有job时为true/0/0/[]，即使worker也为空。不加入每个worker只能接一个job的限制。\r\n\r\n局部检查命令：`python3 engines/operator_assignment/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\r\n\r\n## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\r\n\r\n所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\r\n`run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\r\nattention是组件名去重排序列表：对每个result，blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入；不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false。每个组件至多列一次。\r\n\r\n## best_cost=10**9; best_assign=None\n    for choice in itertools.product(range(len(workers)), repeat=n):\n        ok=True\n        loads=[0]*len(workers)\n        for ji,wi in enumerate(choice):\n            w=workers[wi]\n            if jobs[ji]['skill'] not in w['skills']:\n                ok=False;break\n            loads[wi]+=jobs[ji]['duration']\n            if loads[wi]>w['capacity']:\n                ok=False;break\n        if not ok: continue\n        score=sum(workers[wi]['affinity'].get(jobs[ji]['id'],0) for ji,wi in enumerate(choice))\n        cost=sum(workers[wi]['cost']*jobs[ji]['duration'] for ji,wi in enumerate(choice))\n        # tuple list sorted by job\n        pairs=tuple((jobs[ji]['id'],workers[wi]['id']) for ji,wi in sorted(enumerate(choice),key=lambda x:jobs[x[0]]['id']))\n        # compare score max, then cost min, then lex pairs\n        if score>best_score or (score==best_score and cost<best_cost) or (score==best_score and cost==best_cost and (best_assign is None or pairs<best_assign)):\n            best_score=score; best_cost=cost; best_assign=pairs\n    if best_assign is None:\n        return None\n    return best_score,best_cost,best_assign\n\nrandom.seed(0)\nfor t in range(500):\n    nj=random.randint(0,5)\n    nw=random.randint(0,4)\n    skills=['s0','s1']\n    jobs=[]\n    for i in range(nj):\n        jobs.append({'id':f'j{i}','skill':random.choice(skills),'duration':random.randint(1,4)})\n    workers=[]\n    for i in range(nw):\n        ws=random.sample(skills, random.randint(1,2))\n        cap=random.randint(0,8)\n        cost=random.randint(0,3)\n        aff={f'j{j}':random.randint(-2,4) for j in range(nj)}\n        workers.append({'id':f'w{i}','skills':ws,'capacity':cap,'cost':cost,'affinity':aff})\n    payload={'jobs':jobs,'workers':workers}\n    r=transform([payload],{'name':'t','operation':'capacity_assignment','inputs':['x'],'output':'y'})['result']\n    b=brute(jobs,workers)\n    if b is None:\n        assert r['feasible'] is False and r['score']==0 and r['cost']==0 and r['assignments']==[], (jobs,workers,r)\n    else:\n        bs,bc,bp=b\n        assert r['feasible'] is True, (jobs,workers,r,b)\n        assert r['score']==bs and r['cost']==bc, (jobs,workers,r,b)\n        rp=tuple((a['job'],a['worker']) for a in r['assignments'])\n        assert rp==bp, (jobs,workers,r,b,rp,bp)\nprint('random operator tests OK')\nPY\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}

succs[u].append(v)\n    # topo via kahn\n    indeg={i:len(preds[i]) for i in ids}\n    ready=sorted([i for i in ids if indeg[i]==0])\n    topo=[]\n    while ready:\n        u=ready.pop(0); topo.append(u)\n        for v in sorted(succs[u]):\n            indeg[v]-=1\n            if indeg[v]==0:\n                ready.append(v); path={}\n    for u in topo:\n        if not preds[u]:\n            finish[u]=dur[u]; path[u]=[u]\n        else:\n            mf=max(finish[p] for p in preds[u])\n            cand=[p for p in preds[u] if finish[p]==mf]\n            bp=min(cand,key=lambda p:path[p])\n            finish[u]=dur[u]+mf; path[u]=path[bp]+[u]\n    ms=max(finish.values())\n    ends=[i for i in ids if finish[i]==ms]\n    cp=path[min(ends,key=lambda i:path[i])]\n    ef={i:finish[i] for i in sorted(ids)}\n    return waves,cp,ms,ef\n\nrandom.seed(1)\nfor t in range(300):\n    n=random.randint(0,7)\n    ids=[f'n{i}' for i in range(n)]\n    nodes=[]\n    for i in range(n):\n        # deps only earlier to ensure acyclic\n        deps=[ids[j] for j in range(i) if random.random()<0.4]\n        if len(set(deps))\"'!=len(deps): deps=sorted(set(deps))\n        nodes.append({'\"'id':ids[i],'duration':random.randint(0,5),'deps':deps})\n    r=transform([{'nodes':nodes}],{'name':'t','operation':'critical_path','inputs':['x'],'output':'y'})['result']\n    w,cp,ms,ef=reference(nodes)\n    assert r['blocked'] is False\n    assert r['waves']==w, (nodes,r,w)\n    assert r['critical_path']==cp, (nodes,r,cp)\n    assert r['makespan']==ms, (nodes,r,ms)\n    assert r['earliest_finish']==ef, (nodes,r,ef)\nprint('random resource_order tests OK')\nPY\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}

ready.sort()\n    assert len(topo)==len(ids)\n    if not ids:\n        return [], [], 0, {}\n    level={}\n    for u in topo:\n        level[u]=0 if not preds[u] else 1+max(level[p] for p in preds[u])\n    maxlev=max(level.values())\n    waves=[sorted([i for i in ids if level[i]==k]) for k in range(maxlev+1)]\n    finish={}; path={}\n    for u in topo:\n        if not preds[u]:\n            finish[u]=dur[u]; path[u]=[u]\n        else:\n            mf=max(finish[p] for p in preds[u])\n            cand=[p for p in preds[u] if finish[p]==mf]\n            bp=min(cand,key=lambda p:path[p])\n            finish[u]=dur[u]+mf; path[u]=path[bp]+[u]\n    ms=max(finish.values())\n    ends=[i for i in ids if finish[i]==ms]\n    cp=path[min(ends,key=lambda i:path[i])]\n    ef={i:finish[i] for i in sorted(ids)}\n    return waves,cp,ms,ef\n\nrandom.seed(1)\nfor t in range(300):\n    n=random.randint(0,7)\n    ids=[f'n{i}' for i in range(n)]\n    nodes=[]\n    for i in range(n):\n        # deps only earlier to ensure acyclic\n        deps=[ids[j] for j in range(i) if random.random()<0.4]\n        if len(set(deps))\"'!=len(deps): deps=sorted(set(deps))\n        nodes.append({'\"'id':ids[i],'duration':random.randint(0,5),'deps':deps})\n    r=transform([{'nodes':nodes}],{'name':'t','operation':'critical_path','inputs':['x'],'output':'y'})['result']\n    w,cp,ms,ef=reference(nodes)\n    assert r['blocked'] is False\n    assert r['waves']==w, (nodes,r,w)\n    assert r['critical_path']==cp, (nodes,r,cp)\n    assert r['makespan']==ms, (nodes,r,ms)\n    assert r['earliest_finish']==ef, (nodes,r,ef)\nprint('random resource_order tests OK')\nPY\"","aggregated_output":"random resource_order tests OK\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

m=len(workers)\n    if n==0:\n        return (0,0,())\n    best=None\n    for choice in itertools.product(range(m), repeat=n):\n        loads=[0]*m; ok=True\n        for ji,wi in enumerate(choice):\n            if jobs[ji]['skill'] not in workers[wi]['skills']: ok=False;break\n            loads[wi]+=jobs[ji]['duration']\n            if loads[wi]>workers[wi]['capacity']: ok=False;break\n        if not ok: continue\n        score=sum(workers[wi]['affinity'].get(jobs[ji]['id'],0) for ji,wi in enumerate(choice))\n        cost=sum(workers[wi]['cost']*jobs[ji]['duration'] for ji,wi in enumerate(choice))\n        pairs=tuple((jobs[ji]['id'],workers[wi]['id']) for ji,wi in sorted(enumerate(choice),key=lambda x:jobs[x[0]]['id']))\n        key=(score,-cost)\n        cur=(score,cost,pairs)\n        if best is None or score>best[0] or (score==best[0] and cost<best[1]) or (score==best[0] and cost==best[1] and pairs<best[2]):\n            best=(score,cost,pairs)\n    return best\n\nrandom.seed(3)\nfor t in range(300):\n    nj=random.randint(0,5); nw=random.randint(0,4)\n    jobs=[]\n    for i in range(nj):\n        jobs.append({'id':f'j{i}','skill':random.choice(['s0','s1']),'duration':random.randint(1,4)})\n    workers=[]\n    for i in range(nw):\n        workers.append({'id':f'w{i}','skills':random.sample(['s0','s1'],random.randint(1,2)),'capacity':random.randint(0,8),'cost':random.randint(0,3),'affinity':{f'j{j}':random.randint(-2,4) for j in range(nj)}})\n    # random subsequent inputs\n    payloads=[{'jobs':jobs,'workers':workers}]\n    selected_ids=random.sample([w['id'] for w in workers], random.randint(0,nw))\n    payloads.append({'name':'up1','result':{'selected':selected_ids}})\n    makespan=random.randint(0,10)\n    payloads.append({'name':'up2','result':{'makespan':makespan}})\n    r=transform(payloads,{'name':'t','operation':'capacity_assignment','inputs':['x','y','z'],'output':'o'})['result']\n    # reference apply filters in order\n    ws=[dict(w) for w in workers]\n    if True:\n        sel=set(selected_ids)\n        ws=[w for w in ws if w['id'] in sel]\n        for w in ws:\n            w['capacity']=min(w['capacity'],makespan)\n    b=brute(jobs,ws)\n    if b is None:\n        assert r['feasible'] is False, (jobs,workers,selected_ids,makespan,r,b)\n    else:\n        assert r['feasible'] is True, (jobs,workers,selected_ids,makespan,r,b)\n        assert r['score']==b[0] and r['cost']==b[1], (jobs,workers,selected_ids,makespan,r,b)\n        rp=tuple((a['job'],a['worker']) for a in r['assignments'])\n        assert rp==b[2], (jobs,workers,selected_ids,makespan,r,rp,b)\nprint('random operator filter tests OK')\nPY\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}
